# 项目: faith-evidence-ie

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: cqa1-21977
- SSH 账户: root（调用 ssh_execute / submit_training_job 时传入 account="root"）
- SSH 连接命令: ssh connect.cqa1.seetacloud.com  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/structfuse


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
## 任务：审查 run_m1_extraction.sh 参数正确性

服务器 cqa1-21977，项目路径 /root/structfuse/

### 背景
M1 全量提取脚本 run_m1_extraction.sh 已准备好，但需要确认参数正确后才能在 P2b 通过后立即启动。

### 检查项

1. **读取脚本**（ssh_read_only）：
   ```bash
   cat /root/structfuse/scripts/run_m1_extraction.sh
   ```

2. **确认以下参数**：
   - workers 数量：应为 8（CPU 并行）
   - 输出路径：必须在 /root/autodl-tmp/（数据盘），不是根分区
   - 断点续传：脚本是否支持 resume（跳过已完成的 repo）
   - SWE-smith metadata 路径：确认 metadata 文件存在且条目数正确（预期 ~50K-59K instances）
   - CodeQL binary 路径：确认可用
   - 超时设置：单 repo 是否有 timeout 防止卡住

3. **验证依赖文件存在**（ssh_read_only）：
   ```bash
   # metadata 文件
   ls -la /root/structfuse/mvp_data/swesmith_metadata*.json* 2>/dev/null || ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/*metadata* 2>/dev/null
   wc -l &lt;metadata_file>  # 或 python -c "import json; print(len(json.load(open('...'))))"
   
   # CodeQL
   which codeql 2>/dev/null || ls /root/codeql*/codeql 2>/dev/null
   
   # batch_extract.py 的 --help
   cd /root/structfuse && python extraction/batch_extract.py --help 2>&1 | head -30
   ```

4. **磁盘空间预估**：
   - 每个 repo 的 CodeQL database 约 50-200MB，全量 5K repos 可能需要 500GB-1TB 临时空间
   - 检查 /root/autodl-tmp/ 可用空间
   - 确认脚本是否逐个处理后清理 database（不堆积）

5. **报告**：
   - 脚本当前参数是否正确
   - 需要修改的地方（列出具体行号和建议修改）
   - 磁盘空间是否够用
   - 预估全量提取耗时

    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. or path.endswith(\\\"/\\\" + f):\\n            return f\\n    basename = os.path.basename(path)\\n    candidates = [f for f in all_py_files if os.path.basename(f) == basename]\\n    if len(candidates) os.path.exists(repo_dir):\\n        return repo_dir\\n    url = f\\\"https://github.com/{github_repo}.git\\\"\\n    cmd = [\\\"git\\\", \\\"clone\\\", url, repo_dir]\\n    try:\\n ## Summary of findings:

### Script contents (run_m1_extraction.sh):
```bash
#!/bin/bash
set -e
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base

cd /root/structfuse

OUTPUT_DIR="/root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/m1_extraction"
mkdir -p "$OUTPUT_DIR"

python extraction/batch_extract.py \
  --metadata /root/autodl-tmp/swe_smith_metadata.jsonl \


```

Finishing the extraction script...

### Check results:

The worker count is set to 4 when the task specifies 8, though with 192 CPUs available this seems conservative—though I should consider that CodeQL creates separate databases for each worker, which could affect resource allocation.

Looking at how the workers interact with CodeQL: each of the 4 worker processes spawns CodeQL with `--threads=0`, meaning each invocation tries to use all available cores. So all 4 workers would be competing for the same 192 CPUs simultaneously. CPU-wise this should be manageable, but disk I/O and memory could become bottlenecks.

The `--timeout 300` parameter is the CodeQL timeout per repository, not a global timeout, so each analysis gets 5 minutes to complete.

The output directory is correctly set to the data disk, and the script supports resuming by checking if output files already exist and reusing cloned repositories. The metadata file has about 59K instances as expected.

CodeQL is installed at `/opt/codeql/codeql`, but this path isn't in the system PATH, so the script calling `codeql` without an absolute path could be problematic.

More critically, disk space is a major constraint—with only 31GB available but needing roughly 66GB minimum to store ~222 repos and their CodeQL databases concurrently, and since neither the cloned repos nor the databases are cleaned up after processing, the script will likely run out of space partway through. QL DBs are stored at `repos_dir/repo__name_codeql_db` and never deleted. So all 222 repos + 222 DBs will accumulate.

7. **Timeout**: 300s for CodeQL operations. Reasonable.

### Issues to fix:

1. **CRITICAL: CodeQL not in PATH** — Need to add `export PATH="/opt/codeql:$PATH"` to the script
2. **CRITICAL: Workers = 4, should be 8** per task requirements (but consider: CodeQL uses --threads=0 which means all cores; with 8 workers each spawning multi-thread CodeQL, this could be fine on 192 CPUs)
3. **CRITICAL: Disk space insufficient** — Only 31GB available, need ~66-100GB. The script doesn't clean up repos or CodeQL DBs after processing a group.
4. **No DB cleanup**: CodeQL DBs accumulate in repos_dir and should be removed after each group finishes processing.

The script estimates runtime at 222 groups × 120s plus 59,136 instances × 30s, which works out to roughly 500 hours serially. With 8 workers that drops to about 62.5 hours, or 125 hours with 4 workers. The actual time will depend on how many instances remain after filtering for Python-only analysis, which the script calculates and reports at runtime.

| 超时设置 | **OK** | `--timeout 300` (5min/repo CodeQL DB 创建)，clone 超时硬编码 600s |
| `--python-only` | **OK** | 只处理纯 Python patch 的 instance |

### 需要修改的问题

#### 1. **CRITICAL: CodeQL 不在 PATH 中**
- CodeQL binary 在 `/opt/codeql/codeql`，但系统 PATH 不含 `/opt/codeql`
- `batch_extract.py` L98 直接调用 `"codeql"`，运行时会报 `FileNotFoundError`
- **修复**：脚本开头加 `export PATH="/opt/codeql:$PATH"`

#### 2. **CRITICAL: 磁盘空间严重不足**
- `/root/autodl-tmp/` 仅剩 **31GB**（总 169GB，已用 139GB）
- ~222 个唯一 (repo, commit) 需要 clone repo + 创建 CodeQL DB，且**代码不清理**：
  - repos 保留在 `repos_dir/` 不删除（L328: 如果存在就复用）
  - CodeQL DB 保留在 `repos_dir/repo__name_codeql_db` 不删除（L501-503）
- 保守估计 222 repos (~50-200MB each) + 222 DBs (~50-200MB each) = **40-80GB**，远超 31GB
- **修复**：需要清理已有数据腾出空间，或在 `process_repo_group()` 末尾添加 CodeQL DB 清理逻辑（处理完一个 group 后删除该 group 的 DB）

#### 3. **Workers = 4，要求是 8**
- 服务器有 192 CPU，当前 `--workers 4` 偏保守
- 但注意：CodeQL 用 `--threads=0`（使用全部 CPU），多 worker 并行时每个 CodeQL 进程都抢全部 CPU
- **建议**：改为 `--workers 8`，同时将 CodeQL `--threads` 改为固定值（如 24=192/8）以避免竞争。但 `--threads` 在 `batch_extract.py` 里是硬编码的（L103: `"--threads=0"`），需改代码或接受 CPU 竞争

### 耗时预估

脚本内置估算公式（L620-624）：
- 每 group 120s DB 创建 + 每 instance 30s 查询
- 222 groups × 120s + N_instances × 30s（N 取决于 `--python-only` 过滤后剩余数量）
- 假设过滤后 ~50K instances：222×120 + 50000×30 = 26,640 + 1,500,000 ≈ **424h serial**
- 4 workers ≈ **106h**，8 workers ≈ **53h**

实际会快于此估算——Beniget fallback 比 CodeQL 快得多，且 resume 可跳过已完成部分。

### 总结：需要 3 项修改才能启动

1. 脚本加 `export PATH="/opt/codeql:$PATH"`
2. 清理磁盘或加 CodeQL DB 自动清理逻辑（至少需要 50GB+ 可用空间）
3. `--workers 4` → `--workers 8`
